<h2>Price and yield history</h2>
<p class="data-vintage">The line plots one point per trade date, the day's last print; the whisker at each point spans the day's high and low prints. The line breaks across gaps longer than 30 days. Fund marks are squares at each period end, joined by the dashed line. Volume is the day total of amounts reported to TRACE; capped reports count at the cap. Scroll or drag on the chart to zoom. One axis per view; a fund mark is a period-end valuation, not a trade price. This bond has 7,976 stored trades; beyond the most recent 90 days the chart and tape show the last trade of each week (1,422 points). The full tape is in the program dataset.</p>

<p class="flow-note">Sides are the reporting dealer&rsquo;s, as FINRA disseminates them: &ldquo;dealer bought&rdquo; means the dealer reporting the trade bought; for a trade with a customer, a dealer sale is a customer purchase. FINRA disseminates inter-dealer trades on the sell side only, so the split is not a measure of investor buying versus selling. TRACE caps reported size at $1MM+ for non-investment-grade bonds; capped trades are counted at the cap, so all volumes are minimums. Counts reflect the tape&rsquo;s current state: cancelled trades are excluded, and corrected trades are counted once, at their corrected values.</p>

(function(){
  var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.tape-filter input[type=checkbox]');
  cbs.forEach(function(cb){ cb.addEventListener('change', function(){
